- **autor.auto-review-loop**: Autonomous multi-round research review loop. Repeatedly reviews via codex-bridge, implements fixes, and re-reviews until positive assessment or max rounds reached. ([llv22/AutoResearchWithEyes](https://github.com/llv22/AutoResearchWithEyes/blob/HEAD/commands/autor.auto-review-loop.md) · ★5)

Orchestrates research-lit → idea-creator → novelty-check → research-reviewer to go from a broad research direction to validated, pilot-tested ideas. Use when user says "idea discovery pipeline" or wants the complete idea exploration workflow. ([llv22/AutoResearchWithEyes](https://github.com/llv22/AutoResearchWithEyes/blob/HEAD/commands/autor.idea-discovery.md) · ★5)
- **autor.paper-writing**: Workflow 3: Full paper writing pipeline. Orchestrates paper-plan → paper-figure → paper-write → paper-compile → paper-improver to go from a narrative report to a polished, submission-ready PDF. Use when user says "write paper pipeline", "paper writing", or wants the complete paper generation workflow. ([llv22/AutoResearchWithEyes](https://github.com/llv22/AutoResearchWithEyes/blob/HEAD/commands/autor.paper-writing.md) · ★5)
- **autor.research-pipeline**: Full research pipeline: Workflow 1 (idea discovery) → implementation → Workflow 2 (auto review loop). Goes from a broad research direction to validated, reviewed research. Use when user says "full pipeline", "end-to-end research", or wants the complete autonomous research lifecycle. Does NOT include paper writing (Workflow 3) — invoke /autor.paper-writing separately after this completes. ([llv22/AutoResearchWithEyes](https://github.com/llv22/AutoResearchWithEyes/blob/HEAD/commands/autor.research-pipeline.md) · ★5)

- **axioms**: Axioms are the named, stable **categories of recurring critique**: a spec is prose for humans and the reviewer; axioms are the enumerable units metrics aggregate over. An axiom is a bucket, never a rule — the norm lives in the spec its `derived_from` points at; the axiom names the issue the reviewers keep finding against it. They are born from real critiques, accepted by a human at curate (where e ([zarpay/praxis-cli](https://github.com/zarpay/praxis-cli/blob/HEAD/site/commands/axioms.md) · ★18)
